R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}R^{n}}=\lim_{R\to+\infty}\frac{\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}{\{{\rho=R}\}}{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}{\{{\rho=R}\}}{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}{\{{\rho=R}\}}{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}{\{{\rho=R}\}}{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{3.07002pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-3.07002pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.82002pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.82002pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.24501pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.24501pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.70833pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.70833pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}R^{n-1}}

where
L
is the link of the cone
(M,g)
is asymptotic to.

One can easily show that
\det(g)=\det(\hat{g})(1+o(1))=\rho^{2(n-1)}\det(g_{L})(1+o(1))
. Hence, for every
\varepsilon>0
there exists
R_{\varepsilon}\geq 1
such that

(2.31)

\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle L$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle L$}}\right|$}}(1-\varepsilon)\frac{(R^{n}-R_{\varepsilon}^{n})}{n}\leq\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\left\{{R_{\varepsilon}\leq\rho\leq R}\}}{\left\{{R_{\varepsilon}\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\left\{{R_{\varepsilon}\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}\leq\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle L$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle L$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle L$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle L$}}\right|$}}(1+\varepsilon)\frac{(R^{n}-R_{\varepsilon}^{n})}{n}

Dividing each term by
\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}R^{n}
one get

(2.32)

\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle L$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle L$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle L$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle L$}}\right|$}}(1-\varepsilon)\frac{(R^{n}-R_{\varepsilon}^{n})}{n\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}R^{n}}\leq\frac{\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\left\{{R_{\varepsilon}\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\left\{{R_{\varepsilon}\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\{{R_{\varepsilon}\leq\rho\leq Since

we obtain

(2.33)

(1-\varepsilon)\frac{\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle L$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq L$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle L$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle L$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle which in turn gives the first identity in (
2.30
) by arbitrariness of
\varepsilon
. We turn to prove the second identity. Since De L’Hôpital rule gives

(2.34)

\lim_{R\to+\infty}\frac{\frac{{\,\rm d}}{{\,\rm d}R}\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{3.07002pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-3.07002pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.82002pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.82002pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.24501pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.24501pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.70833pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.70833pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}R^{n-1}}=\lim_{R\to+\infty}\frac{\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq and

(2.35)

\frac{{\,\rm d}}{{\,\rm d}R}\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}=\frac{{\,\rm d}}{{\,\rm d}R}\int\limits_{1}^{R}\int\limits_{\mathchoice{\left\{{\rho=s}\right\}}{\{{\rho=s}\}}{\left\{{\rho=s}\right\}}{\left\{{\rho=s}\right\}}}\frac{1}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle{\rm D}\rho$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle{\rm D}\rho$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\rm D}\rho$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\rm D}\rho$}}\right|$}}}{\,\rm d}\sigma{\,\rm d}s=\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}{\{{\rho=R}\}}{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}{\{{\rho=R}\}}{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}{\{{\rho=R}\}}{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}{\{{\rho=R}\}}{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}{\left\{{\rho=R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}(1+o(1)),

we conclude the proof. ∎

Coupling this result with
lemma

2.15
one gets that

(2.36)

\lim_{R\to+\infty}\frac{\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle B(o,R)$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle B(o,R)$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle B(o,R)$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle B(o,R)$}}\right|$}}}{R^{n}\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}}=\lim_{R\to+\infty}\frac{\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}{\left\{{1\leq\rho\leq R}\right\}}$}}\right|$}}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}R^{n}}=\frac{\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle L$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle L$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle L$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle L$}}\right|$}}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{3.07002pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-3.07002pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.82002pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.82002pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.24501pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.24501pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.70833pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.70833pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}}

for every
o\in M
. Hence the left hand side limit exists and does not depend on the point
o\in M
. We can finely give the following definition.

Definition 2.17
.

Let
(M,g)
be a complete
\mathscr{C}^{0}
-Asymptotically Conical Riemannian manifold. The Asymptotic Volume Ratio of
(M,g)
is defined as

(2.37)

\AVR(g)=\frac{\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle L$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle L$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle L$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle L$}}\right|$}}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{3.07002pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-3.07002pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.82002pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.82002pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.24501pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.24501pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.70833pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.70833pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}}

where
L
is the link of the cone
(M,g)
is asymptotic to.

In this case
0<\AVR(g)
, but in general
\AVR(g)
could exceed
1
and
\AVR(g)=1
does not imply that
(M,g)
is isometric to the flat Euclidean space.

As already observed in the Introduction, a complete
\mathscr{C}^{0}
-Asymptotically Conical Riemannian manifold is forced to have a finite number of ends.

Lemma 2.18
.

A complete
\mathscr{C}^{0}
-Asymptotically Conical Riemannian manifold
(M,g)
has finitely many ends with connected boundary each of them is differomorphic to
[1,+\infty)\times L_{i}
where
L_{i}
is a connected component of the link of the asymptotic cone.

Since
L
is a compact hypersurface, it has a finite number of connected component. Each end with respect to
K
is therefore diffeomorphic to a cone on a connected component of
L
. ∎

As already mentioned in the Introduction, given
E_{1},\ldots,E_{N}
the ends of
M
, each
E_{i}
is
\mathscr{C}^{0}
-Asymptotically Conical and we can define the Asymptotic Volume ratio of
E_{i}
as

(2.38)

\AVR(g;E_{i})=\lim_{R\to+\infty}\frac{\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle B(o,R)\cap E_{i}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle B(o,R)\cap E_{i}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle B(o,R)\cap E_{i}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.06995pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.06995pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle B(o,R)\cap E_{i}$}}\right|$}}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{B}^{n}$}}\right|$}}R^{n}}=\frac{\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle L_{i}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle L_{i}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle L_{i}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.06995pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.06995pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle L_{i}$}}\right|$}}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{3.07002pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-3.07002pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.82002pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.82002pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.24501pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.24501pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.70833pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.70833pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}},

for every
i=1,\ldots,N
, where as a above
L_{i}
denotes a connected component of the link of the asymptotic cone. Moreover, the Asymptotic Volume Ratio of
(M,g)
splits as

(2.39)

\AVR(g)=\sum_{i=1}^{N}\AVR(g;E_{i})

where
\AVR(g)>0
is the Asymptotic Volume Ratio of
(M,g)
.

If
\Ric_{L}\geq(n-2)g_{L}
, then the cone
([1,+\infty)\times L,\hat{g})
has nonnegative Ricci curvature and in particular
\AVR(g;E_{i})\leq 1
for every
i=1,\ldots,k
. This condition is automatically true if the manifold is
\mathscr{C}^{0}
-Asymptotically Conical and
\Ric
satisfies (
1.3
), thanks to the following lemma.

Lemma 2.19
.

Let
(M,g)
be a complete
\mathscr{C}^{0}
-Asymptotically Conical Riemannian manifold. Suppose that
\Ric_{g}\geq-f(\mathop{\rm dist}\nolimits(x,o))
for some nonnegative smooth function
f(t)=o(1)
as
t\to+\infty
, for some
o\in M
. Then
\Ric_{\hat{g}}\geq 0
, where
\hat{g}={\,\rm d}\rho^{2}+\rho^{2}g_{L}
is the asymptotical conic metric of
g
and
L
is the link of the limit cone. In particular,
\AVR(g)\leq N
where
N
is the number of connected component of the link
L
.

By
lemma

2.14
we can assume that
f
is a function of
\rho
. Let
\omega_{s}
be as in (
2.20
), we denote by
g_{(s)}
the metric
s^{-2}\omega_{s}^{*}g
on
[1/s,+\infty)\times L
. It is easy to prove that
([1/s,+\infty)\times L,\mathop{\rm dist}\nolimits_{g_{(s)}},x)
converges in the pointed-Gromov-Hausdorff topology to
([0,+\infty)\times L,\mathop{\rm dist}\nolimits_{\hat{g}},x)
for some
x\in\mathchoice{\left\{{\rho=2}\right\}}{\{{\rho=2}\}}{\left\{{\rho=2}\right\}}{\left\{{\rho=2}\right\}}
. Since

(2.40)

\lim_{s\to+\infty}\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle B(x,1)$}}\right|_{g_{(s)}}$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle B(x,1)$}}\right|_{g_{(s)}}$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle B(x,1)$}}\right|_{g_{(s)}}$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle B(x,1)$}}\right|_{g_{(s)}}$}}=\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle B(x,1)$}}\right|_{\hat{g}}$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle B(x,1)$}}\right|_{\hat{g}}$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle B(x,1)$}}\right|_{\hat{g}}$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle B(x,1)$}}\right|_{\hat{g}}$}}

by
[
DG18
, Theorem 1.2]

([1/s,+\infty)\times L,\mathop{\rm dist}\nolimits_{g_{(s)}},\mu_{g_{(s)}},x)
converges to
([0,+\infty)\times L,\mathop{\rm dist}\nolimits_{\hat{g}},\mu_{\hat{g}},x)
in the pointed-measured-Gromov-Hausdorff topology. By
[
GMS15
, Theorem 7.2]

\Ric_{\hat{g}}\geq-f(s)
for every
s
, hence
\Ric_{\hat{g}}\geq 0
. In particular,
\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle L_{i}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle L_{i}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle L_{i}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.06995pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.06995pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle L_{i}$}}\right|$}}\leq\mathchoice{\raisebox{3.07002pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-3.07002pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.82002pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.82002pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.24501pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.24501pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.70833pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.70833pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$}}\right|$}}
, hence
\AVR(g)\leq N
by
eqs.

2.39
and
2.38
.∎

In
[
MRS21
, Theorem 1.7]
the authors guarantee the existence of the (weak) IMCF starting at
\Omega\subseteq M
with smooth boundary whenever the Ricci curvature satisfies a nondecreasing lower bound and a global
L^{1}
-Sobolev Inequality is in force, that is

(2.41)

\left(\;\int\limits_{M}\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\,\rm d}\mu\right)^{\frac{n-1}{n}}\leq\mathrm{C}_{S}\int\limits_{M}\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle{\rm D}\varphi$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle{\rm D}\varphi$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\rm D}\varphi$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\rm D}\varphi$}}\right|$}}{\,\rm d}\mu

for every
\varphi\in\mathrm{Lip}_{c}(M)
where
\mathrm{C}_{S}
is some finite constant depending only on the geometry of the manifold.

It is well known that the existence of a finite constant
\mathrm{C}_{S}
for the
L^{1}
-Sobolev Inequality is equivalent to the existence of a positive Isoperimetric constant, which is a
\mathrm{C}_{\rm Iso}>0
such that

(2.42)

\mathrm{C}_{\rm Iso}\leq\frac{\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\partial K$}}\right|^{n}$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\partial K$}}\right|^{n}$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\partial K$}}\right|^{n}$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\partial K$}}\right|^{n}$}}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle K$}}\right|^{n-1}$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle K$}}\right|^{n-1}$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle K$}}\right|^{n-1}$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K$}}\right|^{n-1}$}}}

for every compact domain
K
(see
[
FF60
, Remark 6.6]
or
[
SY94
, pp. 89-90]
).

Riemannian manifolds with nonnegative Ricci curvature and Euclidean Volume Growth satisfies both (
2.41
) and (
2.42
) as observed by
[
Var85
]
(see also
[
Car94
]
and
[
Heb99
, Theorem 8.4]
). It is then plausible that these inequalities hold also when the manifold asymptotically behaves as a cone with nonnegative Ricci curvature.

Proposition 2.20
.

Let
(M,g)
be a
\mathscr{C}^{0}
-Asymptotically Conical Riemannian manifold. Suppose that
\Ric_{g}\geq-f(\mathop{\rm dist}\nolimits(x,o))
for some nonnegative smooth function
f(t)=o(1)
as
t\to+\infty
, for some
o\in M
. Then
(M,g)
admits a global
L^{1}
-Sobolev Inequality (
2.41
) for some finite constant
\mathrm{C}_{S}
or equivalently it has a positive isoperimetric constant
\mathrm{C}_{\rm Iso}>0
.

In virtue of
[
PST14
, Theorem 3.2]
it is enough to prove that a
L^{1}
-Sobolev Inequality is satisfied outside some compact set. By
lemma

2.18

(M,g)
has only a finite number of ends each of them corresponding to one connected component of the link
L
. Thus, we can assume that
(M,g)
has only one Asymptotically Conical end
E
. By
lemma

2.19
,
E
asymptotically behaves as a cone with nonnegative Ricci curvature, that satisfies the
L^{1}
-Sobolev Inequality for some constant
\hat{C}
. Suppose by contradiction that for every compact
K\subset E
, the
L^{1}
-Sobolev Inequality is not satisfied on
E\smallsetminus K
. Since the metric
g
converges to the metric
\hat{g}={\,\rm d}\rho^{2}+\rho^{2}g_{L}
, for every
\varepsilon>0
there exists a compact
K_{\varepsilon}
such that

(2.43)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.38611pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-0.38611pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\;\int\limits_{M}\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\,\rm d}{\mu_{g}}-\int\limits_{M}\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\,\rm d}{\mu_{\hat{g}}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{0.38611pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-0.38611pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\;\int\limits_{M}\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\,\rm d}{\mu_{g}}-\int\limits_{M}\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\,\rm d}{\mu_{\hat{g}}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{0.48112pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-0.48112pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\;\int\limits_{M}\mathchoice{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.225pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.225pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-0.875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\,\rm d}{\mu_{g}}-\int\limits_{M}\mathchoice{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.225pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.225pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-0.875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\,\rm d}{\mu_{\hat{g}}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{-0.87498pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{0.87498pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\;\int\limits_{M}\mathchoice{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-0.875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{0.625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-0.625pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\,\rm d}{\mu_{g}}-\int\limits_{M}\mathchoice{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-0.875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{0.625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-0.625pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\,\rm d}{\mu_{\hat{g}}}$}}\right|$}}\leq\varepsilon\int\limits_{M}\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varphi$}}\right|^{\frac{n}{n-1}}$}}{\,\rm d}{\mu_{\hat{g}}}

and

(2.44)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.38611pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-0.38611pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\;\int\limits_{M}\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle{\rm D}\varphi$}}\right|_{g}$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle{\rm D}\varphi$}}\right|_{g}$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\rm D}\varphi$}}\right|_{g}$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\rm D}\varphi$}}\right|_{g}$}}{\,\rm d}{\mu_{g}}-\int\limits_{M}\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle{\rm D}\varphi$}}\right|_{\hat{g}}$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle{\rm D}\varphi$}}\right|_{\hat{g}}$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\rm D}\varphi$}}\right|_{\hat{g}}$}}{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\rm D}\varphi$}}\right|_{\hat{g}}$}}{\,\rm d}{\mu_{\hat{g}}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{0.38611pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-0.38611pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\;\int\limits_{M}\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle{\rm d}{\mu_{g}}-\int\limits_{M}\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle{\rm D}\varphi$}}\right|_{\hat{g}}$}}{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle{\rm D}\varphi$}}\right|_{\hat{g}}$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\rm d}{\mu_{\hat{g}}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{-0.38333pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{0.38333pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\;\int\limits_{M}\mathchoice{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle{\rm D}\varphi$}}\right|_{g}$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle{\rm D}\varphi$}}\right|_{\hat{g}}$}}{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle\left|\raisebox{-1.75pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle{\rm d}{\mu_{\hat{g}}}$}}\right|$}}{\raisebox{-1.30554pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\left|\raisebox{1.30554pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\;\int\limits_{M}\mathchoice{\raisebox{1.25pt}{$\displaystyle\left|\raisebox{-1.25pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle{\rm D}\varphi$}}\right|_{g}$}}{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptstyle\left|\raisebox{-0.875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\rm
